[EVENT: IRQ48913]
Date: 2023-10-01
Country: Iraq | Location: Baghdad - Rusafa
Type: Protests > Protest with intervention | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Iraq (2022-2026) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Baghdad Today; Basnews; Kurdistan24; ROJ News; Shafaaq News [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 October 2023, hundreds of protesters tried to block the road toward Al-Tayaran Square with rubber barriers in Tahrir Square in Baghdad - Rusafa. The security forces prevented them from blocking the road and intervened to disperse the protest by chasing groups of demonstrators, launching sound bombs in the air, and clashing with the protesters causing several injuries. The reason for the protest was to commemorate the fourth anniversary of the October 2019 protests, and demand for the release of their sons and relatives detained in Iraqi prisons, as well as revealing the killers of activists and protesters during the 2019 demonstrations. There were no fatalities.

---

[EVENT: IRQ48940]
Date: 2023-10-01
Country: Iraq | Location: Amara
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 October 2023, teachers protested in front of the directorate of education in Amara city. The protesters area teachers assigned to rural schools and demanded to be transferred to schools in Amara city. The protesters stated some of them have been assigned to rural schools for 10 years and have to travel over 100km to reach work.

Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ROJ News [Regional]

DESCRIPTION:

[EVENT: IRQ49195]
Date: 2023-10-17
Country: Iraq | Location: As Samawah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 17 October 2023, farmers protested in front of the Ministry of Agriculture building in As Samawah city. The protesters demanded the provision of fertilizers, seeds, and fuel at what they called reasonable process. They also demanded the distribution of aid to rice farmers affected by floods. The protesters also called for the postponement of the sprinkler irrigation requirement until next year and the provision of such irrigation tools to farmers. The Iraq government had previously announced plans to make agricultural subsidies contingent upon farmers adapting modern irrigation methods such as sprinkler and drip irrigation.

Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ANF News; ROJ News [Regional-International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 5 November 2023, Turkish army shelled PKK positions in Gire Orte area in Al Amadiya district, Duhok. HPG (PKK) claimed that Turkish forces used bombs containing poisonous gas during the shelling, but this was not confirmed by other sources. Casualties unknown.
